This file contains a packed representation of the entire repository's contents.

The content is organized as follows:

<usage_guidelines>
- This file should be treated as read-only. Any changes should be made to the
  original repository files, not this packed version.
- When processing this file, use the file path to distinguish
  between different files in the repository.
- Be aware that this file may contain sensitive information. Handle it with
  the same level of security as you would the original repository.
</usage_guidelines>

<notes>

- Binary files are not included in this packed representation. Please refer to the Repository Structure section for a complete list of file paths, including binary files
- Files matching patterns in .gitignore are excluded
- Files matching default ignore patterns are excluded
- Files are sorted by Git change count (files with more changes are at the bottom)
</notes>

<file path="scripts/download-assets.mjs">
import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';

// Standard free Pexels / Unsplash photography; see PHOTO_CREDITS.md.
const photos = {
  rice: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1631515243349-e0cb75fb8d3a',
  pulao: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1694004599545-9f25feaf665a',
  seekh: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/29173089/pexels-photo-29173089.jpeg',
  wings: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/28674546/pexels-photo-28674546.jpeg',
  shawarma: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/8018079/pexels-photo-8018079.jpeg',
  pizza: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1513104890138-7c749659a591',
  burger: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1568901346375-23c9450c58cd',
  chicken: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/3926125/pexels-photo-3926125.jpeg',
  strawberry: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/5338141/pexels-photo-5338141.jpeg',
  mango: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/4955257/pexels-photo-4955257.jpeg',
  banana: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/4051764/pexels-photo-4051764.jpeg',
  interior: 'https://images.pexels.com/photos/8146875/pexels-photo-8146875.jpeg',
};
await mkdir('public/images', { recursive: true });
const results = await Promise.allSettled(
  Object.entries(photos).map(async ([name, url]) => {
    for (const width of [640, 1440]) {
      const response = await fetch(`${url}?auto=format&fit=crop&w=${width}&q=82&fm=webp`);
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`${name}: HTTP ${response.status}`);
      await writeFile(`public/images/${name}-${width}.webp`, new Uint8Array(await response.arrayBuffer()));
    }
    console.log(`Downloaded ${name}`);
  }),
);
for (const result of results) if (result.status === 'rejected') console.error(result.reason.message);
if (results.some((result) => result.status === 'rejected')) process.exitCode = 1;
</file>

<file path="scripts/download-demo-assets.mjs">
import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import sharp from 'sharp';

// Licensed stock for the reusable Pizza Max demo, not photographs of a real business.
// Source pages and photographer credits are recorded in PHOTO_CREDITS_DEMO.md.
const photos = {
  'pizza-cheese': '15550301',
  'pizza-veggie': '16203831',
  'pizza-pepperoni': '6488932',
  'pizza-chicken': '33458049',
  'ice-cream': '1683546',
  'chicken-kebab': '7474082',
};
const directory = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../public/images/demo');
await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });

const results = await Promise.allSettled(
  Object.entries(photos).map(async ([name, id]) => {
    const url = `https://images.pexels.com/photos/${id}/pexels-photo-${id}.jpeg?auto=compress&cs=tinysrgb&w=1800`;
    const response = await fetch(url);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`${name}: HTTP ${response.status}`);
    const original = Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer());
    await Promise.all(
      [640, 1440].map(async (width) => {
        const output = await sharp(original)
          .rotate()
          .resize({ width, withoutEnlargement: true })
          .webp({ quality: 82, effort: 5 })
          .toBuffer();
        await writeFile(path.join(directory, `${name}-${width}.webp`), output);
      }),
    );
    console.log(`Prepared ${name}`);
  }),
);
for (const result of results) if (result.status === 'rejected') console.error(result.reason.message);
if (results.some((result) => result.status === 'rejected')) process.exitCode = 1;
</file>

<file path="scripts/download-venue-assets.mjs">
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import sharp from 'sharp';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
const records = JSON.parse(
  await fs.readFile(new URL('../docs/venue-photo-provenance.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'),
);
const destination = new URL('../public/images/venue/', import.meta.url);
await fs.mkdir(destination, { recursive: true });
for (const record of records) {
  const response = await fetch(record.download);
  if (!response.ok) throw Error(`${record.id}: ${response.status}`);
  const source = Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer());
  for (const width of [640, 1440])
    await sharp(source)
      .rotate()
      .resize({ width, withoutEnlargement: true })
      .webp({ quality: 80, effort: 5 })
      .toFile(fileURLToPath(new URL(`${record.id}-${width}.webp`, destination)));
  console.log(`Prepared ${record.id}`);
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/Brand.tsx">
import { BUSINESS } from '../config/business';
import { useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';

export function Brand({ linked = true }: { linked?: boolean }) {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();
  const content = (
    <>
      {BUSINESS.logo && (
        <img
          className="original-logo"
          src={BUSINESS.logo}
          alt={BUSINESS.name[language]}
          width="1254"
          height="1254"
        />
      )}
      <span className="wordmark" aria-hidden={BUSINESS.logo ? true : undefined}>
        <span>
          {BUSINESS.shortName[language]}
          <i aria-hidden="true">.</i>
        </span>
        <small>{t.restaurant}</small>
      </span>
    </>
  );
  return linked ? (
    <a className="brand" href="#home" aria-label={BUSINESS.name[language]}>
      {content}
    </a>
  ) : (
    <span className="brand">{content}</span>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/ContactLink.tsx">
import { useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { BUSINESS, phoneHref, directionsUrl } from '../config/business';
import { useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { Phone, MapPin } from 'lucide-react';

/** A real contact link when configured; an honest, working demo interaction otherwise. */
export function ContactLink({
  kind = 'phone',
  className,
  children,
  'aria-label': label,
}: {
  kind?: 'phone' | 'directions';
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  'aria-label'?: string;
}) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const titleId = useId();
  const url = kind === 'phone' ? phoneHref : directionsUrl;
  if (url)
    return (
      <a
        className={className}
        href={url}
        aria-label={label}
        {...(kind === 'directions' ? { target: '_blank', rel: 'noreferrer' } : {})}
      >
        {children}
      </a>
    );
  return (
    <>
      <button className={className} aria-label={label} onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        {children}
      </button>
      {open && (
        <Dialog titleId={titleId} className="contact-demo-dialog" onClose={() => setOpen(false)}>
          <span className="contact-demo-icon">
            {kind === 'phone' ? <Phone size={28} /> : <MapPin size={28} />}
          </span>
          <span className="eyebrow">{BUSINESS.demo ? t.demoLabel : t.nav.contact}</span>
          <h2 id={titleId}>
            {BUSINESS.demo ? (kind === 'phone' ? t.demoTitle : t.demoMapTitle) : t.contactPending}
          </h2>
          <p>{BUSINESS.demo ? (kind === 'phone' ? t.demoPhoneBody : t.demoMapBody) : t.confirmHours}</p>
          <button className="button primary" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
            {t.demoClose}
          </button>
        </Dialog>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/Dialog.tsx">
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode, type KeyboardEventHandler } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';

export function Dialog({
  titleId,
  className = '',
  onClose,
  onKeyDown,
  children,
}: {
  titleId: string;
  className?: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler<HTMLDialogElement>;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const { t, language } = useLanguage();
  useEffect(() => {
    const dialog = ref.current!;
    const previous = document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null;
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    dialog.showModal();
    return () => {
      dialog.close();
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      if (previous?.isConnected) previous.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    };
  }, []);
  return createPortal(
    <dialog
      ref={ref}
      className={`dialog ${className}`}
      dir={language === 'ps' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr'}
      aria-labelledby={titleId}
      onCancel={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopPropagation();
        onClose();
      }}
      onKeyDown={(event) => {
        event.stopPropagation();
        if (event.key === 'Tab') {
          const focusable = [
            ...event.currentTarget.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
              'button:not(:disabled), a[href], input:not(:disabled), textarea:not(:disabled), [tabindex="0"]',
            ),
          ].filter((element) => element.getClientRects().length > 0);
          const first = focusable[0],
            last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
          const active = document.activeElement;
          if (event.shiftKey && (active === first || !focusable.includes(active as HTMLElement))) {
            event.preventDefault();
            last?.focus();
          } else if (!event.shiftKey && active === last) {
            event.preventDefault();
            first?.focus();
          }
        }
        onKeyDown?.(event);
      }}
      onClick={(event) => {
        if (event.target !== event.currentTarget) return;
        const bounds = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (
          event.clientX < bounds.left ||
          event.clientX > bounds.right ||
          event.clientY < bounds.top ||
          event.clientY > bounds.bottom
        )
          onClose();
      }}
    >
      <button className="icon-button dialog-close" onClick={onClose} aria-label={t.close} autoFocus>
        <X size={22} />
      </button>
      {children}
    </dialog>,
    document.body,
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/Header.tsx">
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowUpRight, Globe2, MapPin, Menu, Phone, ShoppingBag, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { BUSINESS, NAVIGATION } from '../config/business';
import { useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';
import { Brand } from './Brand';
import { useOrder } from './OrderContext';
import { ContactLink } from './ContactLink';

export function Header() {
  const { t, language, toggle, number } = useLanguage();
  const { count, open } = useOrder();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 24);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', handler, { passive: true });
    handler();
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handler);
  }, []);
  return (
    <>
      <a className="skip-link" href="#main">
        {t.skip}
      </a>
      <div className="topbar">
        <div className="container">
          <span>
            {BUSINESS.demo ? (
              <>
                <b>{t.demoLabel}</b> · {t.welcome}
              </>
            ) : (
              BUSINESS.name[language]
            )}
          </span>
          <a href="#contact">
            <MapPin size={12} />
            {BUSINESS.demo ? t.topLocation : BUSINESS.locality[language]}
            <ArrowUpRight size={12} />
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
      <header className={`header ${scrolled ? 'scrolled' : ''}`}>
        <div className="container header-inner">
          <Brand />
          <nav className="desktop-nav" aria-label={t.openNav}>
            {NAVIGATION.map((key) => (
              <a key={key} href={`#${key}`}>
                {t.nav[key]}
              </a>
            ))}
          </nav>
          <div className="header-actions">
            <button className="language-button" onClick={toggle} aria-label={t.languageSwitch}>
              <Globe2 size={16} />
              <span lang={language === 'en' ? 'ps' : 'en'}>{language === 'en' ? 'پښتو' : 'English'}</span>
            </button>
            <button className="order-button" onClick={open} aria-label={`${t.order} (${number(count)})`}>
              <ShoppingBag size={17} />
              <span>{t.order}</span>
              <b>{number(count)}</b>
            </button>
            <button
              className="icon-button mobile-toggle"
              aria-label={mobileOpen ? t.closeNav : t.openNav}
              aria-expanded={mobileOpen}
              aria-controls="mobile-navigation"
              onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)}
            >
              {mobileOpen ? <X size={23} /> : <Menu size={23} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {mobileOpen && (
          <nav
            id="mobile-navigation"
            className="mobile-nav"
            aria-label={t.openNav}
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                setMobileOpen(false);
                document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.mobile-toggle')?.focus();
              }
            }}
          >
            {NAVIGATION.map((key, index) => (
              <a key={key} href={`#${key}`} onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}>
                <span className="nav-number" aria-hidden="true">
                  0{index + 1}
                </span>
                {t.nav[key]}
                <ArrowUpRight size={18} />
              </a>
            ))}
            <ContactLink className="mobile-phone">
              <Phone size={17} />
              <bdi>{BUSINESS.phone ?? t.demoPhone}</bdi>
            </ContactLink>
          </nav>
        )}
      </header>
    </>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/OrderContext.tsx">
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { MENU, type PizzaSize } from '../data/menu';
import { addLine, changeQuantity, orderSubtotal, sanitizeOrder, type OrderLine } from '../lib/order';
import { DELIVERY_MODES, type ServiceMode } from '../config/delivery';
import { interpolate, useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';
import { STORAGE_KEYS } from '../config/business';

function readSaved() {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.order) ?? '{}');
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}
const OrderContext = createContext<{
  lines: OrderLine[];
  mode: ServiceMode;
  setMode: (mode: ServiceMode) => void;
  add: (id: string, size?: PizzaSize) => void;
  update: (key: string, quantity: number) => void;
  subtotal: number;
  count: number;
  open: () => void;
  close: () => void;
  isOpen: boolean;
} | null>(null);

export function OrderProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<OrderLine[]>(() => sanitizeOrder(readSaved()?.lines));
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<ServiceMode>(() => {
    const saved = readSaved()?.mode;
    return DELIVERY_MODES.some((mode) => mode.id === saved) ? saved : DELIVERY_MODES[0].id;
  });
  const [isOpen, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<{ id: string; time: number } | null>(null);
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.order, JSON.stringify({ lines, mode }));
    } catch {
      /* Storage is optional. */
    }
  }, [lines, mode]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (toast) {
      const id = window.setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2600);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(id);
    }
  }, [toast]);
  const add = (id: string, size?: PizzaSize) => {
    if (MENU.find((item) => item.id === id)?.price == null) return;
    setLines((current) => addLine(current, id, size));
    setToast({ id, time: Date.now() });
  };
  return (
    <OrderContext.Provider
      value={{
        lines,
        mode,
        setMode,
        add,
        update: (key, qty) => setLines((current) => changeQuantity(current, key, qty)),
        subtotal: orderSubtotal(lines),
        count: lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + line.quantity, 0),
        isOpen,
        open: () => setOpen(true),
        close: () => setOpen(false),
      }}
    >
      {children}
      <div className={`toast ${toast ? 'visible' : ''}`} role="status" aria-live="polite">
        {toast && (
          <>
            <Check size={18} />
            <span>
              {interpolate(t.toastAdd, { name: MENU.find((item) => item.id === toast.id)!.name[language] })}
            </span>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </OrderContext.Provider>
  );
}
export function useOrder() {
  const value = useContext(OrderContext);
  if (!value) throw new Error('OrderProvider is required');
  return value;
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/OrderDrawer.tsx">
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Check,
  Copy,
  Download,
  Info,
  Minus,
  Phone,
  Plus,
  ShoppingBag,
  Trash2,
  Truck,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { BUSINESS } from '../config/business';
import { DELIVERY_MODES, deliveryStatus } from '../config/delivery';
import { MENU } from '../data/menu';
import { interpolate, useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';
import { lineKey, orderTicket, unitPrice } from '../lib/order';
import { Brand } from './Brand';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { useOrder } from './OrderContext';
import { Photo } from './Photo';
import { ContactLink } from './ContactLink';

export function OrderDrawer() {
  const { t, language, number, money } = useLanguage();
  const { lines, mode, setMode, subtotal, count, update, isOpen, close } = useOrder();
  const [view, setView] = useState<'cart' | 'ticket'>('cart');
  const [copyFallback, setCopyFallback] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<'copied' | 'copyFailed' | 'downloaded' | null>(null);
  const status = deliveryStatus(subtotal, mode);
  const ticket = orderTicket(lines, mode, language, t);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) {
      setView('cart');
      setFeedback(null);
      setCopyFallback(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) document.getElementById('order-dialog-title')?.focus({ preventScroll: true });
  }, [view, isOpen]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (feedback) {
      const timeout = window.setTimeout(() => setFeedback(null), 4000);
      return () => window.clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
  }, [feedback]);
  async function copy() {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(ticket);
      setFeedback('copied');
    } catch {
      setCopyFallback(true);
      setFeedback('copyFailed');
    }
  }
  function download() {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(['\uFEFF', ticket], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' }));
    const anchor = document.createElement('a');
    anchor.href = url;
    anchor.download = `${BUSINESS.id}-order-${language}.txt`;
    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
    anchor.click();
    anchor.remove();
    window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    setFeedback('downloaded');
  }
  if (!isOpen) return null;
  return (
    <Dialog
      titleId="order-dialog-title"
      className={view === 'cart' ? 'order-drawer' : 'ticket-dialog'}
      onClose={close}
    >
      {view === 'cart' ? (
        <>
          <div className="drawer-heading">
            <span className="eyebrow">{t.drawerEyebrow}</span>
            <h2 id="order-dialog-title" tabIndex={-1}>
              {t.drawerTitle}
            </h2>
            <p>{interpolate(t.itemCount, { count: number(count) })}</p>
          </div>
          {lines.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="empty-order">
              <div className="empty-order-icon">
                <ShoppingBag size={36} strokeWidth={1} />
              </div>
              <h3>{t.emptyTitle}</h3>
              <p>{t.emptyBody}</p>
              <a className="button primary" href="#menu" onClick={close}>
                {t.backMenu}
                <ArrowRight className="directional" size={17} />
              </a>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="order-lines">
                {lines.map((line) => {
                  const item = MENU.find((item) => item.id === line.id)!;
                  return (
                    <article className="order-line" key={lineKey(line)}>
                      <Photo image={item.image} sizes="80px" />
                      <div className="order-line-main">
                        <h3>{item.name[language]}</h3>
                        <p>
                          {line.size && `${t.sizes[line.size]} · `}
                          {money(unitPrice(line)!)}
                        </p>
                        <div className="quantity-control">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => update(lineKey(line), line.quantity - 1)}
                            aria-label={`${t.decrease}: ${item.name[language]}`}
                          >
                            <Minus size={14} />
                          </button>
                          <span>{number(line.quantity)}</span>
                          <button
                            disabled={line.quantity >= 99}
                            onClick={() => update(lineKey(line), line.quantity + 1)}
                            aria-label={`${t.increase}: ${item.name[language]}`}
                          >
                            <Plus size={14} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="order-line-end">
                        <strong>{money(unitPrice(line)! * line.quantity)}</strong>
                        <button
                          className="remove-button"
                          onClick={() => update(lineKey(line), 0)}
                          aria-label={`${t.remove}: ${item.name[language]}`}
                        >
                          <Trash2 size={15} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </article>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <fieldset className="delivery-options">
                <legend>{t.serviceMode}</legend>
                {DELIVERY_MODES.map((option) => (
                  <label
                    className={`delivery-option ${mode === option.id ? 'selected' : ''}`}
                    key={option.id}
                  >
                    <input
                      type="radio"
                      name="service-mode"
                      value={option.id}
                      checked={mode === option.id}
                      onChange={() => setMode(option.id)}
                    />
                    <span>
                      <strong>{t.modes[option.id]}</strong>
                      <small>
                        {interpolate(t.modeDescriptions[option.id], { minimum: money(option.minimum) })}
                      </small>
                    </span>
                    {option.id === 'pickup' ? <ShoppingBag size={18} /> : <Truck size={19} />}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </fieldset>
              <div className={`delivery-feedback ${status.eligible ? 'eligible' : ''}`} aria-live="polite">
                <p>
                  {status.eligible ? <Check size={17} /> : <Truck size={17} />}
                  <span>
                    {mode === 'pickup'
                      ? t.pickupEligible
                      : status.eligible
                        ? t.eligible
                        : interpolate(t.remaining, { amount: money(status.remaining) })}
                  </span>
                </p>
                {mode !== 'pickup' && (
                  <div
                    className="delivery-progress"
                    role="progressbar"
                    aria-label={t.progress}
                    aria-valuenow={Math.round(status.progress)}
                    aria-valuemin={0}
                    aria-valuemax={100}
                  >
                    <span style={{ width: `${status.progress}%` }} />
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <div className="drawer-summary">
                <div className="summary-total">
                  <span>{t.subtotal}</span>
                  <strong data-testid="subtotal">{money(subtotal)}</strong>
                </div>
                <button
                  className="button primary full-width"
                  disabled={!status.eligible}
                  onClick={() => setView('ticket')}
                >
                  {t.ticket}
                  <ArrowRight className="directional" size={18} />
                </button>
                {!status.eligible && <p className="minimum-helper">{t.minimumHelper}</p>}
                <p className="call-notice">
                  <Info size={15} />
                  <span>{BUSINESS.demo ? t.demoTicketDisclaimer : t.callNotice}</span>
                </p>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </>
      ) : (
        <div className="ticket-content">
          <Brand linked={false} />
          <span className="eyebrow">{t.ticketEyebrow}</span>
          <h2 id="order-dialog-title" tabIndex={-1}>
            {t.ticketTitle}
          </h2>
          <div className="ticket-service">
            <span>{t.ticketService}</span>
            <strong>{t.modes[mode]}</strong>
          </div>
          <div className="ticket-lines">
            {lines.map((line) => {
              const item = MENU.find((item) => item.id === line.id)!;
              return (
                <div className="ticket-line" key={lineKey(line)}>
                  <span>
                    <strong>
                      {number(line.quantity)} × {item.name[language]}
                    </strong>
                    <small>
                      {line.size ? `${t.sizes[line.size]} · ` : ''}
                      {money(unitPrice(line)!)}
                    </small>
                  </span>
                  <b>{money(unitPrice(line)! * line.quantity)}</b>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          {mode !== 'pickup' && (
            <div className="ticket-delivery">
              <span>{t.freeDelivery}</span>
              <span>{money(0)}</span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="summary-total">
            <span>{t.total}</span>
            <strong>{money(subtotal)}</strong>
          </div>
          <p className="ticket-disclaimer">
            <Info size={17} />
            {BUSINESS.demo ? t.demoTicketDisclaimer : t.ticketDisclaimer}
          </p>
          <ContactLink className="button primary full-width call-now">
            <Phone size={19} />
            <span>
              {BUSINESS.demo ? t.demoCall : t.callNow}
              {BUSINESS.phone && <small dir="ltr">{BUSINESS.phone}</small>}
            </span>
            <ArrowUpRightIcon />
          </ContactLink>
          <div className="ticket-actions">
            <button className="button secondary" onClick={copy}>
              <Copy size={16} />
              {t.copy}
            </button>
            <button className="button secondary" onClick={download}>
              <Download size={16} />
              {t.download}
            </button>
          </div>
          <p role="status" className="ticket-feedback">
            {feedback && t[feedback]}
          </p>
          {copyFallback && (
            <textarea
              className="ticket-copy-text"
              readOnly
              value={ticket}
              aria-label={t.ticketText}
              onFocus={(event) => event.target.select()}
            />
          )}
          <button
            className="text-link edit-order"
            onClick={() => {
              setView('cart');
              setFeedback(null);
            }}
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} className="directional" />
            {t.editOrder}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </Dialog>
  );
}
function ArrowUpRightIcon() {
  return <ArrowRight className="directional" size={20} />;
}

export function StickyOrder() {
  const { count, subtotal, open, isOpen } = useOrder();
  const { t, money, number } = useLanguage();
  if (!count || isOpen) return null;
  return (
    <button className="sticky-order" onClick={open}>
      <span>
        <ShoppingBag size={18} />
        <b>{number(count)}</b>
        {t.viewOrder}
      </span>
      <strong>
        {money(subtotal)}
        <ArrowRight className="directional" size={18} />
      </strong>
    </button>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/Photo.tsx">
import { useState } from 'react';
import { UtensilsCrossed } from 'lucide-react';
import { type SiteImage } from '../config/siteImages';
import { useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';

export function Photo({
  image,
  className = '',
  priority = false,
  sizes = '(max-width: 600px) 100vw, (max-width: 1000px) 50vw, 33vw',
}: {
  image?: SiteImage;
  className?: string;
  priority?: boolean;
  sizes?: string;
}) {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div
      className={`photo ${loaded ? 'is-loaded' : ''} ${!image || failed ? 'photo-fallback' : ''} ${className}`}
    >
      {image && !failed ? (
        <img
          src={image.src}
          srcSet={image.srcSet}
          sizes={sizes}
          width={image.width}
          height={image.height}
          alt={image.alt[language]}
          loading={priority ? 'eager' : 'lazy'}
          fetchPriority={priority ? 'high' : 'auto'}
          decoding="async"
          style={{ objectPosition: image.position }}
          onLoad={() => setLoaded(true)}
          onError={() => setFailed(true)}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="photo-placeholder">
          <UtensilsCrossed size={28} strokeWidth={1} />
          <span>{failed ? t.imageUnavailable : t.photoSoon}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

<file path="src/config/delivery.ts">
export const DELIVERY_MODES = [
  { id: 'nearby', minimum: 500, fee: 0 },
  { id: 'city', minimum: 1000, fee: 0 },
  { id: 'pickup', minimum: 0, fee: 0 },
] as const;
export type ServiceMode = (typeof DELIVERY_MODES)[number]['id'];

export function deliveryStatus(subtotal: number, mode: ServiceMode) {
  const rule = DELIVERY_MODES.find((item) => item.id === mode)!;
  return {
    ...rule,
    remaining: Math.max(0, rule.minimum - subtotal),
    eligible: mode === 'pickup' || subtotal >= rule.minimum,
    progress: rule.minimum === 0 ? 100 : Math.min(100, Math.max(0, (subtotal / rule.minimum) * 100)),
  };
}
</file>

<file path="src/config/metadata.ts">
import { BUSINESS } from './business.ts';
import { en } from '../i18n/en.ts';
import { ps } from '../i18n/ps.ts';

/** Also used by Vite so built HTML follows the same business settings as React. */
export function getMetadata(language: 'en' | 'ps' = 'en') {
  const t = language === 'en' ? en : ps;
  const fill = (value: string) => value.replaceAll('{restaurant}', BUSINESS.name[language]);
  return {
    title: fill(BUSINESS.demo ? t.pageTitle : t.pageTitleLive),
    description: fill(BUSINESS.demo ? t.pageDescription : t.pageDescriptionLive),
  };
}

<file path="src/i18n/format.ts">
import type { Language } from './LanguageContext';

// Explicit digit substitution also supports browsers with a reduced ICU locale set.
export function formatNumber(value: number, language: Language) {
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US').format(value);
  return language === 'ps'
    ? formatted.replace(/\d/g, (digit) => '۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹'[Number(digit)]).replaceAll(',', '٬')
    : formatted;
}
</file>

<file path="src/i18n/LanguageContext.tsx">
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { en, type Translation } from './en';
import { ps } from './ps';
import { formatNumber } from './format';
import { BUSINESS, STORAGE_KEYS } from '../config/business';
export type Language = 'en' | 'ps';
const LanguageContext = createContext<{
  language: Language;
  t: Translation;
  toggle: () => void;
  money: (value: number) => string;
  number: (value: number) => string;
} | null>(null);

export function LanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState<Language>(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.language) === 'ps' ? 'ps' : 'en';
    } catch {
      return 'en';
    }
  });
  const base = language === 'en' ? en : ps;
  const values: Record<string, string> = {
    restaurant: BUSINESS.name[language],
    shortName: BUSINESS.shortName[language],
  };
  const replaceBrand = (text: string) =>
    text.replace(/\{(restaurant|shortName)\}/g, (_, key: string) => values[key]);
  const t = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(base).map(([key, value]) => [
      key,
      typeof value === 'string'
        ? replaceBrand(value)
        : Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).map(([k, v]) => [k, replaceBrand(v)])),
    ]),
  ) as Translation;
  if (!BUSINESS.demo) {
    t.pageTitle = t.pageTitleLive;
    t.pageDescription = t.pageDescriptionLive;
    t.mapNote = t.mapNoteLive;
    t.footerBottom = t.footerBottomLive;
    t.menuPriceNote = t.menuPriceNoteLive;
    t.deliveryBody = t.deliveryBodyLive;
  }
  const number = (value: number) => formatNumber(value, language);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.lang = language;
    document.documentElement.dir = language === 'ps' ? 'rtl' : 'ltr';
    document.title = t.pageTitle;
    document.querySelector('meta[name="description"]')?.setAttribute('content', t.pageDescription);
    document.querySelector('meta[property="og:title"]')?.setAttribute('content', t.pageTitle);
    document.querySelector('meta[property="og:description"]')?.setAttribute('content', t.pageDescription);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.language, language);
    } catch {
      /* Private browsing may disable storage. */
    }
  }, [language, t.pageTitle, t.pageDescription]);
  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider
      value={{
        language,
        t,
        toggle: () => setLanguage((v) => (v === 'en' ? 'ps' : 'en')),
        number,
        money: (value) => `${number(value)} ${t.currency}`,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useLanguage() {
  const context = useContext(LanguageContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error('LanguageProvider is required');
  return context;
}

export function interpolate(text: string, values: Record<string, string | number>) {
  return text.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, key: string) => String(values[key] ?? ''));
}
</file>

<file path="src/lib/order.ts">
import { MENU, type PizzaSize } from '../data/menu';
import { BUSINESS } from '../config/business';
import type { ServiceMode } from '../config/delivery';
import type { Translation } from '../i18n/en';
import type { Language } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';
import { formatNumber } from '../i18n/format';

export type OrderLine = { id: string; size?: PizzaSize; quantity: number };
export const lineKey = (line: Pick<OrderLine, 'id' | 'size'>) => `${line.id}:${line.size ?? ''}`;
export const unitPrice = (line: OrderLine) => {
  const item = MENU.find((item) => item.id === line.id);
  if (!item || item.price === null) return null;
  if (item.sizes) return line.size ? (item.sizes[line.size] ?? null) : null;
  return line.size ? null : item.price;
};
export function sanitizeOrder(value: unknown): OrderLine[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  const result: OrderLine[] = [];
  for (const line of value) {
    if (
      !line ||
      typeof line !== 'object' ||
      typeof line.id !== 'string' ||
      !Number.isInteger(line.quantity) ||
      line.quantity < 1 ||
      unitPrice(line) === null
    )
      continue;
    const existing = result.find((item) => lineKey(item) === lineKey(line));
    if (existing) existing.quantity = Math.min(99, existing.quantity + line.quantity);
    else
      result.push({
        id: line.id,
        ...(line.size ? { size: line.size } : {}),
        quantity: Math.min(99, line.quantity),
      });
  }
  return result;
}
export const orderSubtotal = (lines: OrderLine[]) =>
  sanitizeOrder(lines).reduce((sum, line) => sum + unitPrice(line)! * line.quantity, 0);
export function addLine(lines: OrderLine[], id: string, size?: PizzaSize) {
  return sanitizeOrder([...lines, { id, size, quantity: 1 }]);
}
export function changeQuantity(lines: OrderLine[], key: string, quantity: number) {
  return sanitizeOrder(lines.map((line) => (lineKey(line) === key ? { ...line, quantity } : line)));
}
export function orderTicket(lines: OrderLine[], mode: ServiceMode, language: Language, t: Translation) {
  const format = (n: number) => formatNumber(n, language);
  const money = (n: number) => `${format(n)} ${t.currency}`;
  return [
    BUSINESS.name[language],
    t.ticketTitle,
    '────────────────────────',
    ...sanitizeOrder(lines).map((line) => {
      const item = MENU.find((item) => item.id === line.id)!;
      return `${format(line.quantity)} × ${item.name[language]}${line.size ? ` (${t.sizes[line.size]})` : ''} · ${money(unitPrice(line)!)} = ${money(unitPrice(line)! * line.quantity)}`;
    }),
    '────────────────────────',
    `${t.ticketService}: ${t.modes[mode]}`,
    `${t.total}: ${money(orderSubtotal(lines))}`,
    BUSINESS.demo ? t.demoLabel : (BUSINESS.phone ?? t.contactPending),
    BUSINESS.address[language],
    '',
    BUSINESS.demo ? t.demoTicketDisclaimer : t.ticketDisclaimer,
  ].join('\n');
}

<file path="PHOTO_CREDITS_DEMO.md">
# Pizza Max demo photography

These stock photographs are retained as unused alternatives. The current menu uses the 53 generated illustrations documented in [docs/menu-image-provenance.json](docs/menu-image-provenance.json). Neither set shows a verified Pizza Max business or establishes the exact appearance of a real restaurant's dishes.

All six source pages below identify their photographs as free Pexels stock. The [Pexels License](https://www.pexels.com/license/) permits free use and modification, including websites and templates. Photographer attribution is included here even though it is not required. Do not imply photographer or pictured-brand endorsement, resell these images as unaltered prints, or redistribute them as a stock-photo collection. Sources and license checked on 23 October 2026.

| Local filename prefix in `public/images/demo/` | Photograph and photographer                                                                                                                                         | Suggested use                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `pizza-cheese`                                 | [Close-up of Pizza with Cheese and Basil](https://www.pexels.com/photo/close-up-of-pizza-with-cheese-and-basil-15550301/) — Deacon Medak                            | Cheese pizza illustration; mozzarella and basil on a dark surface.                                     |
| `pizza-veggie`                                 | [Close-up of a Pizza with Vegetables](https://www.pexels.com/photo/close-up-of-a-pizza-with-vegetables-16203831/) — Nomad Ashwin                                    | Vegetable pizza illustration; peppers, olives, and cheese.                                             |
| `pizza-pepperoni`                              | [Yummy pizza with pepperoni slices on dark background](https://www.pexels.com/photo/yummy-pizza-with-pepperoni-slices-on-dark-background-6488932/) — Piotr Arnoldes | Pepperoni or spicy pizza illustration; pepperoni and green chili pieces. Meat type is not verified.    |
| `pizza-chicken`                                | [Delicious Barbecue Chicken Pizza on Wooden Table](https://www.pexels.com/photo/delicious-barbecue-chicken-pizza-on-wooden-table-33458049/) — pedro furtado         | Chicken pizza illustration with a creamy sauce drizzle.                                                |
| `ice-cream`                                    | [Scoops of different ice cream on plate](https://www.pexels.com/photo/scoops-of-different-ice-cream-on-plate-1683546/) — Valeria Boltneva                           | General dessert section illustration; mixed scoops and mint. Do not label this as one specific flavor. |
| `chicken-kebab`                                | [Close-up of a Chicken Kebab Dish](https://www.pexels.com/photo/close-up-of-a-chicken-kebab-dish-7474082/) — Valeria Boltneva                                       | General chicken grill illustration; grilled skewers with vegetables and dip.                           |

Each image is stored as 640px and 1440px-wide WebP files, retaining the original aspect ratio. `node scripts/download-demo-assets.mjs` re-downloads and optimizes these assets using Sharp. Files remain local at runtime; there are no requests to photo services when visitors browse the website.

The pre-existing stock images and their sources are documented separately in `PHOTO_CREDITS.md`. Replace image paths and bilingual alt text in `src/config/siteImages.ts` when customizing this template for a real restaurant.
</file>

<file path="PHOTO_CREDITS_VENUE.md">
# Venue photography credits

These seven stock photographs show different venues and people. They illustrate the demo atmosphere; they are not Pizza Max premises, employees, guests, or endorsements. No food-only imagery is used in the gallery.

All selected images are available under the [Pexels license](https://www.pexels.com/license/), checked 26 October 2026. It permits use in websites and templates and image modification; do not redistribute the files as a standalone stock library, imply endorsement, or use identifiable people offensively. Keep the visible demo/stock labels until replacing them with authorized client photography.

Only responsive 640px and 1440px WebP derivatives are served from `public/images/venue/`. Cropping is controlled by CSS; no generative alterations were made. Photographs were chosen for warm lighting, wood finishes and an informal dining atmosphere; they are not a single-venue photo shoot.

| Local prefix              | Photographer | Source                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------- | ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `dining-window-*`         | Oleg Prachuk | [a wooden table and chairs inside the restaurant](https://www.pexels.com/photo/a-wooden-table-and-chairs-inside-the-restaurant-7941458/)            |
| `interior-architecture-*` | Sharath G.   | [cozy modern restaurant interior design](https://www.pexels.com/photo/cozy-modern-restaurant-interior-design-32328742/)                             |
| `dining-tables-*`         | Misbaa eri   | [elegant modern restaurant interior with wooden decor](https://www.pexels.com/photo/elegant-modern-restaurant-interior-with-wooden-decor-32255665/) |
| `exterior-day-*`          | iremonat     | [exterior of a restaurant with tables on the patio](https://www.pexels.com/photo/exterior-of-a-restaurant-with-tables-on-the-patio-13833442/)       |
| `exterior-evening-*`      | Mahmud KARA  | [exterior of a restaurant at dusk](https://www.pexels.com/photo/exterior-of-a-restaurant-at-dusk-23639701/)                                         |
| `kitchen-*`               | Max Avans    | [men working in restaurant kitchen](https://www.pexels.com/photo/men-working-in-restaurant-kitchen-5056628/)                                        |
| `experience-*`            | Mizuno K     | [two men eating at a table](https://www.pexels.com/photo/two-men-eating-at-a-table-13334801/)                                                       |

Exact download URLs, dimensions and retrieval date: `docs/venue-photo-provenance.json`. Restore optimized files with `node scripts/download-venue-assets.mjs`.
</file>

<file path="vite.config.ts">
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import { BUSINESS } from './src/config/business.ts';
import { THEME } from './src/config/theme.ts';
import { getMetadata } from './src/config/metadata.ts';

const escapeHtml = (value: string) =>
  value.replaceAll('&', '&amp;').replaceAll('"', '&quot;').replaceAll('<', '&lt;').replaceAll('>', '&gt;');
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [
    react(),
    {
      name: 'restaurant-metadata',
      transformIndexHtml(html) {
        const { title, description } = getMetadata();
        const content: Record<string, string> = {
          description,
          'og:title': title,
          'og:description': description,
          'theme-color': THEME.primary,
        };
        return html
          .replace(/<title>.*?<\/title>/, `<title>${escapeHtml(title)}</title>`)
          .replace(/<meta\s+(name|property)="([^"]+)"[^>]*>/g, (tag, attribute: string, key: string) =>
            content[key] ? `<meta ${attribute}="${key}" content="${escapeHtml(content[key])}" />` : tag,
          )
          .replace(/<link rel="icon"[^>]*>/, `<link rel="icon" href="${escapeHtml(BUSINESS.favicon)}" />`);
      },
    },
  ],
});

<file path="scripts/audit-accessibility.mjs">
import { chromium } from '@playwright/test';
import AxeBuilder from '@axe-core/playwright';
import { mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
const browser = await chromium.launch({ channel: 'msedge', headless: true });
const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 1440, height: 1000 } });
const page = await context.newPage();
await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' });
await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:5173/');
const results = [];
async function audit(language, view) {
  await page.evaluate(() => document.fonts.ready);
  const { violations } = await new AxeBuilder({ page }).withTags(['wcag2a', 'wcag2aa', 'wcag21aa']).analyze();
  results.push({ language, view, violations });
  console.log(`${language} / ${view}: ${violations.length} violations`);
  if (violations.length)
    console.log(
      JSON.stringify(
        violations.map((v) => ({
          id: v.id,
          impact: v.impact,
          nodes: v.nodes.map((n) => ({ target: n.target, summary: n.failureSummary })),
        })),
        null,
        2,
      ),
    );
}
for (const language of ['en', 'ps']) {
  if (language === 'ps') await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Switch to Pashto' }).click();
  await audit(language, 'page');
  await page.locator('.reservation-button').click();
  await audit(language, 'reservation');
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await page.locator('.gallery-tile').first().click();
  await audit(language, 'gallery');
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  if (language === 'en') await page.locator('.platter-actions .add-button').click();
  await page.locator('.order-button').click();
  await audit(language, 'cart');
  await page.locator('input[value="pickup"]').check();
  await page.locator('.drawer-summary > .button').click();
  await audit(language, 'ticket');
  await page.locator('.dialog-close').click();
}
await browser.close();
await mkdir('.qa', { recursive: true });
await writeFile('.qa/accessibility.json', JSON.stringify(results, null, 2));
if (results.some((result) => result.violations.length)) process.exitCode = 1;

<file path="scripts/capture-preview.mjs">
import { chromium } from '@playwright/test';
import { mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';

await mkdir('.qa', { recursive: true });
const browser = await chromium.launch({ channel: 'msedge' });
try {
  const context = await browser.newContext({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' });
  const page = await context.newPage();
  await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:5173');
  for (const language of ['en', 'ps']) {
    if (language === 'ps') await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Switch to Pashto' }).click();
    for (const width of [1440, 768, 390]) {
      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 1000 });
      await page.evaluate(async () => {
        await document.fonts.ready;
        const images = [...document.images];
        images.forEach((img) => (img.loading = 'eager'));
        await Promise.all(images.map((img) => img.decode().catch(() => {})));
      });
      await page.evaluate(() => window.scrollTo(0, 0));
      await page.screenshot({ path: `.qa/pizza-max-${language}-${width}-hero.png` });
      for (const [section, selector] of Object.entries({
        categories: '.category-previews',
        menu: '#menu',
        platters: '.platter-section',
        desserts: '.juice-section',
        gallery: '#gallery',
        contact: '#contact',
        footer: 'footer',
      })) {
        await page.locator(selector).screenshot({
          path: `.qa/${language}-${width}-${section}.png`,
          style: '.header { visibility: hidden; }',
        });
      }
    }
  }
  console.log(
    'Saved hero, categories, menu, platters, desserts, gallery, contact and footer previews in both languages at desktop, tablet and mobile widths.',
  );
} finally {
  await browser.close();
}

<file path="scripts/verify-menu-images.mjs">
import { readFile, access } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { createServer } from 'vite';
import sharp from 'sharp';

// Load the real configuration: category growth and photo replacements need no script edits.
const server = await createServer({ server: { middlewareMode: true }, appType: 'custom' });
try {
  const { MENU } = await server.ssrLoadModule('/src/data/menu.ts');
  const { SITE_IMAGES } = await server.ssrLoadModule('/src/config/siteImages.ts');
  const ids = MENU.map((item) => item.id);
  if (!ids.length || new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) throw Error('Missing or duplicate menu IDs');
  const fingerprints = new Set();
  let count = 0,
    bytes = 0;
  async function verify(image, label, unique = false) {
    if (!image?.srcSet || !image.alt.en || !image.alt.ps)
      throw Error(`Incomplete image configuration: ${label}`);
    for (const variant of image.srcSet.split(',')) {
      const [url, width] = variant.trim().split(/\s+/);
      if (!url.startsWith('/images/')) throw Error(`Nonlocal image: ${url}`);
      const file = 'public' + url;
      await access(file);
      const data = await readFile(file);
      const meta = await sharp(data).metadata();
      if (meta.width !== parseInt(width) || meta.format !== 'webp')
        throw Error(`Invalid responsive file: ${file}`);
      if (url === image.src && unique) {
        const hash = createHash('sha256').update(data).digest('hex');
        if (fingerprints.has(hash)) throw Error(`Duplicate dish photo: ${label}`);
        fingerprints.add(hash);
      }
      bytes += data.length;
      count++;
    }
  }
  for (const item of MENU) await verify(item.image, item.id, true);
  if (fingerprints.size !== MENU.length) throw Error('A dish source is missing from its srcSet');
  const menuCount = count;
  for (const photo of SITE_IMAGES.gallery) {
    if (!photo.image.src.startsWith('/images/venue/')) throw Error('Food image in venue gallery');
    await verify(photo.image, photo.id);
  }
  console.log(
    `Verified ${MENU.length} distinct menu images / ${menuCount} WebPs, ${SITE_IMAGES.gallery.length} venue photographs / ${count - menuCount} WebPs. ${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB total.`,
  );
} finally {
  await server.close();
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/Gallery.tsx">
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight, Expand } from 'lucide-react';
import { SITE_IMAGES, VENUE_FILTERS } from '../config/siteImages';
import { interpolate, useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';
import { Dialog } from './Dialog';
import { Photo } from './Photo';

const FILTERS = VENUE_FILTERS;
type Filter = (typeof FILTERS)[number];
export function Gallery() {
  const { t, language, number } = useLanguage();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>('all');
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const photos = SITE_IMAGES.gallery.filter((photo) => filter === 'all' || filter === photo.category);
  const index = photos.findIndex((photo) => photo.id === selected);
  const current = index >= 0 ? photos[index] : null;
  const hasPreviews = SITE_IMAGES.gallery.some((photo) => photo.image.preview);
  const hasRealPhotos = SITE_IMAGES.gallery.some((photo) => !photo.image.preview);
  const move = (offset: number) => setSelected(photos[(index + offset + photos.length) % photos.length].id);
  return (
    <section id="gallery" className="gallery-section section-space container">
      <div className="section-heading heading-split">
        <div>
          <span className="eyebrow">{t.galleryEyebrow}</span>
          <h2>{t.galleryTitle}</h2>
        </div>
        <span className="outlined-label">
          {hasRealPhotos ? t.galleryReal : t.galleryPreview}
          <ArrowUpRight size={14} />
        </span>
      </div>
      {hasPreviews && <p className="gallery-note">{hasRealPhotos ? t.galleryMixedNote : t.galleryNote}</p>}
      <div className="filter-list gallery-filters" role="group" aria-label={t.nav.gallery}>
        {FILTERS.map((value) => (
          <button
            key={value}
            onClick={() => setFilter(value)}
            aria-pressed={filter === value}
            className={filter === value ? 'active' : ''}
          >
            {t.galleryCategories[value]}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className={`gallery-grid venue-gallery-grid ${filter !== 'all' ? 'filtered' : ''}`} key={filter}>
        {photos.map((photo) => (
          <button
            key={photo.id}
            className={`gallery-tile gallery-${photo.id}`}
            onClick={() => setSelected(photo.id)}
            aria-label={`${t.viewImage}: ${photo.image.alt[language]}`}
          >
            <Photo image={photo.image} sizes={'(max-width: 760px) 100vw, 50vw'} />
            <span className={`gallery-provenance ${photo.image.preview ? '' : 'is-real'}`}>
              {photo.image.preview ? t.galleryPreview : t.galleryReal}
            </span>
            <span className="gallery-tile-label">
              {t.galleryCategories[photo.category as Exclude<Filter, 'all'>]}
              <Expand size={19} />
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {current && (
        <Dialog
          titleId="gallery-dialog-title"
          className="lightbox"
          onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
          onKeyDown={(event) => {
            if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
              event.preventDefault();
              move(language === 'ps' ? -1 : 1);
            }
            if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
              event.preventDefault();
              move(language === 'ps' ? 1 : -1);
            }
          }}
        >
          <div className="lightbox-top">
            <span id="gallery-dialog-title">{current.image.preview ? t.galleryPreview : t.galleryReal}</span>
            <span aria-live="polite">
              {interpolate(t.imageCount, { current: number(index + 1), total: number(photos.length) })}
            </span>
          </div>
          <Photo image={current.image} key={current.id} priority sizes="90vw" />
          <div className="lightbox-bottom">
            <button className="icon-button" onClick={() => move(-1)} aria-label={t.previous}>
              <ArrowLeft className="directional" />
            </button>
            <p>{current.image.alt[language]}</p>
            <button className="icon-button" onClick={() => move(1)} aria-label={t.next}>
              <ArrowRight className="directional" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </Dialog>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/MenuSection.tsx">
import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import {
  ArrowUpRight,
  Check,
  Minus,
  Phone,
  Plus,
  UtensilsCrossed,
  Search,
  X,
  IceCreamBowl,
  GlassWater,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { CATEGORIES, MENU, type Category, type MenuItem, type PizzaSize } from '../data/menu';
import { SECTIONS } from '../config/sections';
import { ContactLink } from './ContactLink';
import { lineKey } from '../lib/order';
import { interpolate, useLanguage } from '../i18n/LanguageContext';
import { Photo } from './Photo';
import { useOrder } from './OrderContext';

export function AddButton({
  item,
  size,
  compact = false,
}: {
  item: MenuItem;
  size?: PizzaSize;
  compact?: boolean;
}) {
  const { t, language, number } = useLanguage();
  const { lines, add, update } = useOrder();
  const quantity = lines.find((line) => lineKey(line) === lineKey({ id: item.id, size }))?.quantity ?? 0;
  if (item.price === null)
    return (
      <ContactLink className="ask-price">
        <span>{t.askPrice}</span>
        <ArrowUpRight size={16} />
      </ContactLink>
    );
  if (quantity && !compact)
    return (
      <div className="quantity-control" aria-label={`${t.quantity}: ${item.name[language]}`}>
        <button
          onClick={() => update(lineKey({ id: item.id, size }), quantity - 1)}
          aria-label={`${t.decrease}: ${item.name[language]}`}
        >
          <Minus size={15} />
        </button>
        <span aria-live="polite">{number(quantity)}</span>
        <button
          onClick={() => add(item.id, size)}
          disabled={quantity >= 99}
          aria-label={`${t.increase}: ${item.name[language]}`}
        >
          <Plus size={15} />
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  return (
    <button
      className={compact ? 'round-add' : 'add-button'}
      disabled={quantity >= 99}
      onClick={() => add(item.id, size)}
      aria-label={`${t.add}: ${item.name[language]}${size ? ` (${t.sizes[size]})` : ''}`}
    >
      {!compact && <span>{t.add}</span>}
      {compact && quantity ? <Check size={18} /> : <Plus size={compact ? 20 : 16} />}
    </button>
  );
}

export function MenuCard({ item }: { item: MenuItem }) {
  const { t, language, money } = useLanguage();
  const [size, setSize] = useState<PizzaSize>('small');
  const price = item.sizes ? item.sizes[size] : item.price;
  return (
    <article className={`menu-card ${!item.image ? 'menu-card-no-photo' : ''}`} data-dish={item.id}>
      {item.image ? (
        <div className="menu-photo-wrap">
          <Photo image={item.image} />
          {item.tag && <span className="dish-tag">{t.tags[item.tag]}</span>}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <span className="dish-line-icon">
          {item.category === 'dessert' ? (
            <IceCreamBowl size={25} strokeWidth={1} />
          ) : item.category === 'juice' ? (
            <GlassWater size={25} strokeWidth={1} />
          ) : (
            <UtensilsCrossed size={25} strokeWidth={1} />
          )}
        </span>
      )}
      <div className="menu-card-body">
        <h3>{item.name[language]}</h3>
        <p>{item.description[language]}</p>
        {item.sizes && (
          <fieldset className="size-selector">
            <legend className="sr-only">{t.chooseSize}</legend>
            {(Object.keys(item.sizes) as PizzaSize[]).map((value) => (
              <label key={value} className={size === value ? 'selected' : ''}>
                <input
                  type="radio"
                  name={`size-${item.id}`}
                  value={value}
                  checked={size === value}
                  onChange={() => setSize(value)}
                />
                <span>{t.sizes[value]}</span>
              </label>
            ))}
          </fieldset>
        )}
        <div className="menu-card-bottom">
          {price !== null && (
            <span key={price} className="dish-price price-change">
              {money(price)}
            </span>
          )}
          <AddButton item={item} size={item.sizes ? size : undefined} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

export function MenuLink({
  category,
  className,
  children,
}: {
  category: Category;
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <a
      href="#menu"
      className={className}
      onClick={() => window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('restaurant:menu', { detail: category }))}
    >
      {children}
    </a>
  );
}

export function MenuSection() {
  const { t, number } = useLanguage();
  const filtersRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<Category>(SECTIONS.defaultCategory);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(SECTIONS.menuPageSize);
  function select(value: Category) {
    setCategory(value);
    setQuery('');
    setLimit(SECTIONS.menuPageSize);
  }
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (event: Event) => {
      const value = (event as CustomEvent<Category>).detail;
      if (CATEGORIES.includes(value)) select(value);
    };
    window.addEventListener('restaurant:menu', handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('restaurant:menu', handler);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const list = filtersRef.current;
    const active = list?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-pressed="true"]');
    if (!list || !active) return;
    const offset = active.getBoundingClientRect().left - list.getBoundingClientRect().left;
    list.scrollBy({
      left: offset - (list.clientWidth - active.clientWidth) / 2,
      behavior: window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ? 'instant' : 'smooth',
    });
  }, [category, query]);
  const term = query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
  const filtered = MENU.filter((item) =>
    term
      ? [item.name.en, item.name.ps, item.description.en, item.description.ps].some((text) =>
          text.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(term),
        )
      : category === 'all' || item.category === category,
  );
  const visible = filtered.slice(0, limit);
  return (
    <section id="menu" className="menu-section section-space">
      <div className="container">
        <div className="section-heading centered">
          <span className="eyebrow">{t.menuEyebrow}</span>
          <h2>{t.menuTitle}</h2>
          <p>{t.menuBody}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="menu-search">
          <Search size={18} />
          <input
            aria-label={t.searchMenu}
            placeholder={t.searchPlaceholder}
            value={query}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setQuery(event.target.value);
              setLimit(SECTIONS.menuPageSize);
            }}
          />
          {query && (
            <button onClick={() => setQuery('')} aria-label={t.clearSearch}>
              <X size={17} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        <div ref={filtersRef} className="filter-list menu-filters" role="group" aria-label={t.nav.menu}>
          {CATEGORIES.map((value) => (
            <button
              key={value}
              aria-pressed={!term && category === value}
              className={!term && category === value ? 'active' : ''}
              onClick={() => select(value)}
            >
              {t.categories[value]}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="menu-result-count" role="status">
          {interpolate(t.menuResults, { shown: number(visible.length), total: number(filtered.length) })}
        </p>
        <div className={`menu-grid ${category === 'all' || term ? 'menu-list' : ''}`} key={category}>
          {visible.map((item) => (
            <MenuCard key={item.id} item={item} />
          ))}
        </div>
        {!filtered.length && (
          <div className="menu-empty">
            <Search size={28} />
            <h3>{t.noResults}</h3>
            <p>{t.noResultsBody}</p>
          </div>
        )}
        {visible.length < filtered.length && (
          <button
            className="button secondary menu-more"
            onClick={() => setLimit((current) => current + SECTIONS.menuPageSize)}
          >
            {t.showMore}
            <Plus size={17} />
          </button>
        )}
        <div className="menu-footnote">
          <Phone size={14} />
          <p>
            {t.menuPriceNote}
            {MENU.some((item) => item.image?.preview) && <span>{t.photoNote}</span>}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/config/siteImages.ts">
import type { Localized } from './business';

export type SiteImage = {
  src: string;
  srcSet?: string;
  alt: Localized;
  position?: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  preview: boolean;
};

// Each menu item has its own original image. Paths, responsive sizes and alt text
// are centralized here. These generated images illustrate the demo menu only.
const dishPhoto = (id: string, en: string, ps: string, height = 960): SiteImage => ({
  src: `/images/menu/${id}-1440.webp`,
  srcSet: `/images/menu/${id}-640.webp 640w, /images/menu/${id}-1440.webp 1440w`,
  alt: { en: `Illustration: ${en}`, ps: `بېلګه: ${ps}` },
  width: 1440,
  height,
  position: 'center',
  preview: true,
});

const menu = {
  'afghan-tikka-roll': dishPhoto('afghan-tikka-roll', 'Afghan Chicken Tikka Roll', 'افغاني چکن تکه رول'),
  'beef-kabab-roll': dishPhoto('beef-kabab-roll', 'Beef Kabab Roll', 'د غوښې کباب رول'),
  'spicy-chutney-roll': dishPhoto('spicy-chutney-roll', 'Spicy Chutney Roll', 'د تندې چټنۍ رول'),
  'egg-potato-roll': dishPhoto('egg-potato-roll', 'Egg & Potato Roll', 'د هګۍ او کچالو رول'),
  'afghan-crispy-broast': dishPhoto(
    'afghan-crispy-broast',
    'Afghan Style Crispy Broast',
    'افغاني کرسپي بروست',
  ),
  'charcoal-wings': dishPhoto('charcoal-wings', 'Charcoal Grilled Wings', 'د سکرو وزرونه'),
  'spicy-fried-wings': dishPhoto('spicy-fried-wings', 'Spicy Fried Wings', 'تند سره شوي وزرونه'),
  'garlic-broast': dishPhoto('garlic-broast', 'Garlic Broast', 'د هوږې بروست'),

  'special-pizza': dishPhoto('special-pizza', 'Special Pizza', 'فل سپیشل پیزا'),
  'cheese-pizza': dishPhoto('cheese-pizza', 'Cheese Pizza', 'چیز پیزا'),
  'crown-pizza': dishPhoto('crown-pizza', 'Crown Crust Pizza', 'کراون کرسټ پیزا'),
  'tikka-pizza': dishPhoto('tikka-pizza', 'Chicken Tikka Pizza', 'چکن تکه پیزا'),
  'bbq-pizza': dishPhoto('bbq-pizza', 'BBQ Chicken Pizza', 'باربیکیو چکن پیزا'),

<file path="src/config/theme.ts">
/** All brand colors. Components use CSS variables populated from this file. */
export const THEME = {
  paper: '#f7f1e6',
  cream: '#e8dcc7',
  primary: '#e9a90c',
  primaryHover: '#cc8f06',
  ink: '#242321',
  accent: '#76530a',
  muted: '#625b52',
  line: '#dcd0bd',
  soft: '#efe4d2',
  dark: '#242321',
  success: '#3e6744',
  successBg: '#e8efdf',
};
export function applyTheme() {
  const variables = { ...THEME, green: THEME.ink, gold: THEME.accent };
  Object.entries(variables).forEach(([key, value]) =>
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(`--${key}`, value),
  );
  document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]')?.setAttribute('content', THEME.primary);
}
</file>

import type { Localized } from '../config/business';
import { SITE_IMAGES, type SiteImage } from '../config/siteImages';
export const CATEGORIES = [
  'all',
  'pizza',
  'rice',
  'kebab',
  'rolls',
  'burgers',
  'crispy',
  'platter',
  'juice',
  'dessert',
] as const;
export type Category = (typeof CATEGORIES)[number];
export type PizzaSize = 'small' | 'medium' | 'large';
export type MenuItem = {
  id: string;
  name: Localized;
  description: Localized;
  category: Category;
  price: number | null;
  sizes?: Record<PizzaSize, number>;
  image?: SiteImage;
  tag?: 'signature' | 'fromGrill' | 'fresh';
  contents?: Localized[];
};

<file path="src/lib/order.test.ts">
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  addLine,
  changeQuantity,
  lineKey,
  orderSubtotal,
  orderTicket,
  sanitizeOrder,
  unitPrice,
} from './order';
import { deliveryStatus } from '../config/delivery';
import { en } from '../i18n/en';
import { ps } from '../i18n/ps';
import { MENU } from '../data/menu';

describe('phone order calculations', () => {
  it('keeps the complete demo menu and all pizza sizes orderable', () => {
    expect(MENU).toHaveLength(61);
    expect(new Set(MENU.map((item) => item.id)).size).toBe(MENU.length);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.sizes)).toHaveLength(11);
    for (const item of MENU) {
      if (item.sizes)
        for (const size of ['small', 'medium', 'large'] as const)
          expect(orderSubtotal(addLine([], item.id, size))).toBe(item.sizes[size]);
      else expect(orderSubtotal(addLine([], item.id))).toBe(item.price ?? 0);
    }
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'platter').map((item) => item.price)).toEqual([
      400, 700, 1300, 1500,
    ]);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'juice')).toHaveLength(12);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'dessert')).toHaveLength(9);
  });
  it('separates the nine categories and rejects all eight unpriced additions', () => {
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'pizza').every((item) => !!item.sizes)).toBe(true);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'pizza')).toHaveLength(11);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'kebab')).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'rolls')).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'burgers')).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(MENU.filter((item) => item.category === 'crispy')).toHaveLength(6);
    const unpriced = MENU.filter((item) => item.price === null);
    expect(unpriced).toHaveLength(8);
    for (const item of unpriced) expect(addLine([], item.id)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('rejects items whose price is cleared during customization', () => {
    const item = MENU.find((item) => item.id === 'mango')!;
    const price = item.price;
    try {
      item.price = null;
      expect(addLine([], item.id)).toEqual([]);
    } finally {
      item.price = price;
    }
  });
  it('keeps pizza sizes separate and merges identical selections', () => {
    const lines = addLine(
      addLine(addLine([], 'special-pizza', 'small'), 'special-pizza', 'large'),
      'special-pizza',
      'small',
    );
    expect(lines).toEqual([
      { id: 'special-pizza', size: 'small', quantity: 2 },
      { id: 'special-pizza', size: 'large', quantity: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(orderSubtotal(lines)).toBe(860);
  });
  it('recalculates quantities and removes zero-quantity items', () => {
    const lines = addLine(addLine([], 'zinger'), 'shawarma');
    expect(orderSubtotal(lines)).toBe(190);
    const updated = changeQuantity(lines, lineKey(lines[0]), 3);
    expect(orderSubtotal(updated)).toBe(430);
    expect(orderSubtotal(changeQuantity(updated, lineKey(lines[0]), 0))).toBe(70);
  });
  it('rejects unpriced items and corrupt or outdated stored selections', () => {
    expect(
      sanitizeOrder([
        { id: 'special-pizza', quantity: 1 },
        { id: 'special-pizza', size: 'huge', quantity: 2 },
        { id: 'zinger', size: 'large', quantity: 1 },
        { id: 'kabuli-palaw', quantity: -1 },
        { id: 'missing', quantity: 3 },
        { id: 'afghani-kebab', quantity: 1.5 },
        null,
      ]),
    ).toEqual([]);
    expect(sanitizeOrder('corrupt')).toEqual([]);
    expect(unitPrice({ id: 'strawberry', quantity: 1 })).toBe(150);
    expect(orderSubtotal(addLine([], 'banana'))).toBe(120);
    expect(addLine([], 'family-platter')).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('caps restored quantities at 99', () =>
    expect(
      sanitizeOrder([
        { id: 'kabuli-palaw', quantity: 99 },
        { id: 'kabuli-palaw', quantity: 99 },
      ])[0].quantity,
    ).toBe(99));
});
describe('delivery minimums', () => {
  it.each([
    ['nearby', 499, false, 1],
    ['nearby', 500, true, 0],
    ['city', 999, false, 1],
    ['city', 1000, true, 0],
    ['pickup', 0, true, 0],
    ['pickup', 70, true, 0],
  ] as const)('%s at %i AFN', (mode, amount, eligible, remaining) => {
    expect(deliveryStatus(amount, mode)).toMatchObject({ eligible, remaining });
  });
  it('caps progress at 100%', () => expect(deliveryStatus(2000, 'nearby').progress).toBe(100));
});
describe('bilingual tickets', () => {
  it('includes quantities, size, unit price, total and explicit call instructions', () => {
    const ticket = orderTicket([{ id: 'special-pizza', size: 'medium', quantity: 2 }], 'nearby', 'en', en);
    expect(ticket).toContain('2 × Special Pizza (Medium) · 300 AFN = 600 AFN');
    expect(ticket).toContain('Order total: 600 AFN');
    expect(ticket).toContain('Demo ticket only');
    expect(ticket).toContain('Pizza Max');
  });
  it('uses Pashto throughout the ticket', () => {
    const ticket = orderTicket([{ id: 'kabuli-palaw', quantity: 1 }], 'pickup', 'ps', ps);
    expect(ticket).toContain('کابلي پلو');
    expect(ticket).toContain('افغانۍ');
    expect(ticket).toContain(ps.modes.pickup);
    expect(ticket).not.toContain('AFN');
  });
  it('keeps every translation key aligned', () => {
    const keys = (obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ''): string[] =>
      Object.entries(obj).flatMap(([key, value]) =>
        typeof value === 'object'
          ? keys(value as Record<string, unknown>, `${prefix}${key}.`)
          : [`${prefix}${key}`],
      );
    expect(keys(ps).sort()).toEqual(keys(en).sort());
  });
});
</file>

<file path="PHOTO_CREDITS.md">
# Template photography and fonts

The current menu uses 61 individually generated food illustrations, identified on the site as demo imagery. Their exact prompts and provenance are in [docs/menu-image-provenance.json](docs/menu-image-provenance.json). Eight new dish prompts are recorded in [docs/menu-expansion-provenance.json](docs/menu-expansion-provenance.json). The venue hero and gallery use seven stock photographs; see [PHOTO_CREDITS_VENUE.md](PHOTO_CREDITS_VENUE.md). The old dining-room asset below is retained as an unused alternative. The stock food photographs listed below and in [PHOTO_CREDITS_DEMO.md](PHOTO_CREDITS_DEMO.md) remain as unused alternatives. The geometric pizza icon in `public/brand/pizza-max-icon.svg` was created for this demo. Previous client-owned assets are preserved outside `public/` and are not distributed with this template.

## Unsplash — standard free license

[Unsplash license](https://unsplash.com/license). These are standard free photographs, not Unsplash+ assets.

| Local filename | Source                                                                                                                            |
| -------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `pulao-*`      | Ahmad Redai Hashmi, [Afghan pulao on a platter](https://unsplash.com/photos/a-close-up-of-a-plate-of-food-on-a-table-IRZZBK4rO7k) |
| `rice-*`       | Mario Raj, [Chicken biryani](https://unsplash.com/photos/a-white-bowl-filled-with-rice-and-meat-ysmeQt1dzcw)                      |
| `pizza-*`      | [Pizza source image](https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1513104890138-7c749659a591)                                                |
| `burger-*`     | [Burger source image](https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1568901346375-23c9450c58cd)                                               |

The pulao photograph is an illustrative Afghan rice dish, not a promise of an exact menu presentation.
